* *called Me from the womb … He named Me*: The promised servant's prophetic call was like Jeremiah's call (see [Jeremiah 1:5](https://ref.ly/Jer1:5); see also [Galatians 1:15](https://ref.ly/Gal1:15)). It was not a general call but a very specific, personal one (see also [Isaiah 43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1); [45:3–4](https://ref.ly/Isa45:3-Isa45:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 49:1–13 (id: 16427)

This is traditionally seen as the second of four songs about a suffering servant (see study note on Isaiah 42:1–4). The servant's role in suffering becomes clearer (see also [Isaiah 50:4–9](https://ref.ly/Isa50:4-Isa50:9); [52:13–53:12](https://ref.ly/Isa52:13-Isa53:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:1-13
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 42:1-4 (ID: `16339@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Syene (ID: `place:Syene`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Quiver (ID: `realia:Quiver`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Grass (ID: `flora:Grass`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`)

## Isaiah 49:1–55.13 (id: 16428)

God planned to rescue the Israelites from exile. But an important question remained. How could sinful Israel serve the Holy One again?

God would make a way to remove Israel’s sin. He would restore their fellowship with him. This means he would bring them back into a right relationship with himself.

The way would be through the promised Servant. This Servant would come for Israel and for all nations. He was first introduced in [Isaiah 42:1–9](https://ref.ly/Isa42:1-Isa42:9). n this passage, he becomes the main focus.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:1-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Garden of Eden (ID: `place:Eden.2`); Syene (ID: `place:Syene`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Praise (ID: `keyterm:Praise`); Mighty (ID: `keyterm:Mighty.2`); Come On! (ID: `keyterm:ComeOn`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`)

## Isaiah 49:2 (id: 16440)

*My mouth*: The servant's mission would be prophetic (see [Isaiah 11:4](https://ref.ly/Isa11:4); [Ephesians 6:17](https://ref.ly/Eph6:17); [Hebrews 4:12](https://ref.ly/Heb4:12); [Revelation 1:16](https://ref.ly/Rev1:16); [2:12](https://ref.ly/Rev2:12), [16](https://ref.ly/Rev2:16); [19:15](https://ref.ly/Rev19:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Quiver (ID: `realia:Quiver`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`)

## Isaiah 49:3 (id: 16441)

The suffering servant would emerge from Israel (see [Isaiah 41:8](https://ref.ly/Isa41:8)) and would be the true Israel. He would serve as both king ([Isaiah 42:1](https://ref.ly/Isa42:1)) and prophet ([Isaiah 49:1](https://ref.ly/Isa49:1)). He would be anointed with the Spirit of God ([Isaiah 61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1)). God would appoint him ([Isaiah 48:16](https://ref.ly/Isa48:16)) as a faithful witness to God's plans ([43:12](https://ref.ly/Isa43:12); [48:20](https://ref.ly/Isa48:20)).

In his mission to both Israel and other nations ([Isaiah 49:6](https://ref.ly/Isa49:6)), the suffering servant would face rejection and disgrace while remaining faithful, unlike the nation of Israel (see [Isaiah 50:4–9](https://ref.ly/Isa50:4-Isa50:9)). His suffering would be for the sake of God's chosen people. 

Jesus became the ultimate fulfillment of the suffering servant (see study note on Isaiah 52:13–53:12).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:3
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 52:13-53.12 (ID: `16507@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`)

## Isaiah 49:4 (id: 16442)

* *I have labored in vain*: Even though people stubbornly refuse his message, the servant will still reach out to them.
* *My vindication is with the LORD*: Despite discouragement and suffering, the servant will continue to perform his duty faithfully, trusting God for the outcome. God promised to reward the servant’s faithfulness (see also [Isaiah 40:10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:10); [50:8](https://ref.ly/Isa50:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 49:6 (id: 16443)

*a light for the nations*: As the Messiah (God's chosen one), the servant's mission would reach the entire world (see [Isaiah 11:10](https://ref.ly/Isa11:10), [12](https://ref.ly/Isa11:12); [42:6](https://ref.ly/Isa42:6)). The apostle Paul explained his mission to the Gentiles based on this prophecy ([Acts 13:47](https://ref.ly/Acts13:47); [26:23](https://ref.ly/Acts26:23)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 49:7 (id: 16444)

* The promised servant will be disliked and rejected by the nations (see [Isaiah 52:14–53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:14-Isa53:5)) and by his own people (see also [50:6–7](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6-Isa50:7); [53:8–9](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8-Isa53:9)).
* Kings and princes will bow low to show respect and humility to the servant (see also [Isaiah 52:15](https://ref.ly/Isa52:15)). God will lift him up after he suffers. God is loyal to his servant.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`)

## Isaiah 49:8 (id: 16445)

God acts at the right time to achieve his goals ([Acts 1:7](https://ref.ly/Acts1:7); [2 Corinthians 6:2](https://ref.ly/2Cor6:2)). Israel returned to rebuild the land of Israel after the exile in Babylon. The land was restored, and the city was rebuilt, starting with Cyrus’s decree ([Isaiah 44:26–28](https://ref.ly/Isa44:26-Isa44:28)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 49:10–11 (id: 16429)

* Like a shepherd, the Lord in his mercy will guide the returning exiles beside cool waters. He promised to comfort them and meet their needs (see also [Isaiah 40:11](https://ref.ly/Isa40:11); [Psalm 23:1](https://ref.ly/Ps23:1)).
* *roads … highways*: The Lord would open up the way of salvation.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:10-11


## Isaiah 49:12 (id: 16430)

The remnant would come from the north (that is, Babylon and Persia), from the west by sea, and from as far south as Egypt.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Syene (ID: `place:Syene`)

## Isaiah 49:13 (id: 16431)

This hymn of praise is in response to the salvation that the servant would make possible.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 49:14 (id: 16432)

The prophet gave reasons to rejoice ([Isaiah 49:8–13](https://ref.ly/Isa49:8-Isa49:13)), but the exiles still thought about the disaster. Babylon invaded, destroyed Jerusalem*,* and took the people of Judah into exile. There, they felt abandoned and forgotten.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 49:18 (id: 16433)

*as jewelry and put them on like a bride*: Just as a wedding is festive and public, God's restoration of Israel would be visible to everyone (see also [Isaiah 61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 49:19–21 (id: 16434)

* The population growth would extend beyond Israel to include people from other nations who turn to the Lord (see [Isaiah 56:6–8](https://ref.ly/Isa56:6-Isa56:8); [Revelation 7:9](https://ref.ly/Rev7:9)).
* *make room*: The increased population would need space far beyond Israel's traditional borders (see also [Zechariah 2:4](https://ref.ly/Zech2:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:19-21


## Isaiah 49:22 (id: 16436)

*bring your sons … carry your daughters*: The powerful rulers of the nations, some of whom had once harmed children in Israel, would help them return to Zion (see also [Isaiah 60:3–9](https://ref.ly/Isa60:3-Isa60:9)). In other parts of Isaiah, he imagined people from foreign nations coming to Zion to worship the Lord (see [Isaiah 2:2–4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:2-Isa2:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Standard (ID: `realia:Standard`)

## Isaiah 49:23 (id: 16437)

* *you will know that I am the LORD*: The surprising role reversal, with kings and queens serving the humble exiles upon their return, will show God's power to do the unimaginable (see also [Isaiah 60:16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16)).
* The godly will face temporary disgrace during the exile in Babylon but would not ultimately be put to shame*,* as the idolaters and ungodly were.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:23

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 49:24 (id: 16438)

*be snatched … be delivered*: The people considered it nearly impossible that they would be rescued from their oppressors.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:24


## Isaiah 49:25 (id: 16439)

The Lord is like a warrior who fights to protect his children, as promised to Abraham ([Genesis 12:3](https://ref.ly/Gen12:3); see also [Isaiah 54:15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 49:25

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mighty (ID: `keyterm:Mighty.2`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 50:1 (id: 16476)

* God was not responsible for the people's problems; their sin caused their exile (see also [Isaiah 59:1–15](https://ref.ly/Isa59:1-Isa59:15)).
* Jerusalem, the main city of Judah ([Psalm 87:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps87:5-Ps87:6)), was destroyed, and the people of Judah went into exile in 586 BC.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Scroll (ID: `realia:Scroll`)

## Isaiah 50:1–3 (id: 16472)

This argument shows that God was not forced to abandon Israel. Therefore, he could choose to accept the nation back as his people if he wished.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Scroll (ID: `realia:Scroll`); Fish (ID: `fauna:Fish`); Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`)

## Isaiah 50:2 (id: 16477)

* *Why did no one answer*: The people did not respond to God's repeated call to trust him and repent of their sins. They were deaf and stubborn (see [Isaiah 6:9–10](https://ref.ly/Isa6:9-Isa6:10); [29:18](https://ref.ly/Isa29:18); [35:5](https://ref.ly/Isa35:5); [42:18–19](https://ref.ly/Isa42:18-Isa42:19)).
* The Lord came and called the people through the prophets (see also [Isaiah 65:1–3](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1-Isa65:3)).
* *do I lack the strength to deliver you?*: God could rescue Israel and Judah from the Assyrians and Babylonians, but he first had to address his people's sinfulness (see [Isaiah 59:1](https://ref.ly/Isa59:1)).
* *My rebuke dries up the sea*: This refers to the parting of the Red Sea during the exodus from Egypt ([Exodus 14:21–22](https://ref.ly/Exod14:21-Exod14:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Fish (ID: `fauna:Fish`)

## Isaiah 50:3 (id: 16478)

People connected events like darkness and wearing mourning clothes with the day of the Lord ([Isaiah 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10); see study note on Isaiah 2:5–22). The mention of darkness might refer to the plague of darkness in Egypt ([Exodus 10:21](https://ref.ly/Exod10:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:3
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 2:5-22 (ID: `15969@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`)

## Isaiah 50:4 (id: 16480)

The Lord, who never gets tired ([Isaiah 40:28](https://ref.ly/Isa40:28)), sends the servant with good news (see [40:28–31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:28-Isa40:31)) to comfort those who are tired (see study note on Isaiah 40:1–31).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:4
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 40:1-31 (ID: `16290@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 50:4–11 (id: 16479)

This is the third of four servant songs (see study note on Isaiah 42:1–4). The loyal and suffering servant represents an ideal Israel. This image came true in Jesus Christ.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:4-11
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 42:1-4 (ID: `16339@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Moth (ID: `fauna:Moth`)

## Isaiah 50:6 (id: 16481)

*struck Me … tore out My beard … scorn and spittle*: These connections with the experience of Jesus at his crucifixion are too close to not be intentional. Jesus is the true suffering servant (see [Matthew 27:27–31](https://ref.ly/Matt27:27-Matt27:31)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:6


## Isaiah 50:7 (id: 16482)

* Externally, the servant would be disgraced and shamed by others. However, inside himself he would wait calmly for God to prove that he was right (see [Isaiah 29:22](https://ref.ly/Isa29:22); [54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4); [61:7](https://ref.ly/Isa61:7)). He trusted that God would defend him and clear his name.
* *set My face like flint*: The servant would be determined to follow God's will (see [Luke 9:51](https://ref.ly/Luke9:51)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 50:8 (id: 16483)

* *The One who vindicates Me is near*: God defends those who suffer for following his will. Vindication is an act of God where he lifts up those wrongly shamed and punishes the wicked, proud, and arrogant (see also [2 Thessalonians 1:6–9](https://ref.ly/2Thess1:6-2Thess1:9)).
* *confront … a case against Me*: Trust in God’s vindication removes fear and strengthens faith (see [Romans 8:31–34](https://ref.ly/Rom8:31-Rom8:34)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`)

## Isaiah 50:9 (id: 16484)

*Who is there to condemn Me?*: This question expects the answer, “No one!” The servant is confident that no one can prove him guilty.

This statement points forward to the New Testament teaching about justification (God declaring a person to be right with him). Paul speaks about this in [Romans 8:31–35](https://ref.ly/Rom8:31-Rom8:35)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Moth (ID: `fauna:Moth`)

## Isaiah 50:10 (id: 16474)

* To see if someone respects the Lord, observe how they treat God's righteous servant. This idea is similar to the statement Jesus made that no one can reach the Father except through him ([John 14:6](https://ref.ly/John14:6)).
* Those who trust in the Lord have strong hope for the future (see [Isaiah 12:2](https://ref.ly/Isa12:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Isaiah 50:10–11 (id: 16473)

This is a call for response to the servant’s ministry.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:10-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Isaiah 50:11 (id: 16475)

*all you who kindle a fire*: Spiritually complacent people do not respond to God. They may feel comfortable and secure now, but they will soon experience great suffering (see [Isaiah 66:24](https://ref.ly/Isa66:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 50:11


## Isaiah 51:1 (id: 16492)

The rock and the quarry represent Abraham and Sarah ([Isaiah 51:2](https://ref.ly/Isa51:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Pit (ID: `realia:Pit`)

## Isaiah 51:1–8 (id: 16485)

This prophecy urges trust in the Lord. Each of its three parts starts with the phrase *Listen to Me* ([Isaiah 51:1](https://ref.ly/Isa51:1), [4](https://ref.ly/Isa51:4), [7](https://ref.ly/Isa51:7)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:1-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); Garden of Eden (ID: `place:Eden.2`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Praise (ID: `keyterm:Praise`); Sarah (ID: `person:Sarah`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Abraham (ID: `person:Abraham`); Moth (ID: `fauna:Moth`); Mosquito (ID: `fauna:Mosquito`); Wool (ID: `realia:Wool`); Pit (ID: `realia:Pit`)

## Isaiah 51:2 (id: 16496)

*Abraham … I blessed him and multiplied him*: The exiled community had decreased in number. They needed to have faith that God could restore them again to a healthy and thriving population.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Abraham (ID: `person:Abraham`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Sarah (ID: `person:Sarah`)

## Isaiah 51:3 (id: 16497)

*Eden … the garden of the LORD*: God’s salvation will one day reestablish conditions like those in which human beings first lived (see [Genesis 2–3](https://ref.ly/Gen2:1-Gen3:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Garden of Eden (ID: `place:Eden.2`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Praise (ID: `keyterm:Praise`)

## Isaiah 51:4 (id: 16498)

* *a law … My justice*: The servant's work will succeed because justice will guide God's kingdom on earth (see [Isaiah 42:1–4](https://ref.ly/Isa42:1-Isa42:4)).
* *a light to the nations*: The nations will accept the Lord and his revelation (see also [Isaiah 42:6](https://ref.ly/Isa42:6); [49:6](https://ref.ly/Isa49:6); [60:1–3](https://ref.ly/Isa60:1-Isa60:3); [66:19–23](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19-Isa66:23); [Matthew 4:15–16](https://ref.ly/Matt4:15-Matt4:16); [Luke 2:32](https://ref.ly/Luke2:32); [John 1:4–9](https://ref.ly/John1:4-John1:9); [12:35–50](https://ref.ly/John12:35-John12:50); [Acts 13:47](https://ref.ly/Acts13:47)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`)

## Isaiah 51:5 (id: 16499)

God is strong enough to crush his enemies and rescue his people, such as when he rescued Israel in the exodus (see [Isaiah 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9); [Exodus 6:6](https://ref.ly/Exod6:6); [15:16](https://ref.ly/Exod15:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 51:6 (id: 16500)

*die like gnats*: Many wicked people will die on the day of God's judgment.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mosquito (ID: `fauna:Mosquito`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 51:7 (id: 16501)

* *My law in your hearts*: The Lord wants his people to understand, value, and follow his word (see also [Psalm 37:31](https://ref.ly/Ps37:31); [Jeremiah 31:33](https://ref.ly/Jer31:33)).
* Those who follow the Lord often face ridicule and insults (see also [Matthew 5:10–12](https://ref.ly/Matt5:10-Matt5:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`)

## Isaiah 51:9–10 (id: 16502)

* *Awake, awake*: God does not sleep. This prayer asks God to act immediately to save his people (see [Isaiah 52:1](https://ref.ly/Isa52:1); see especially God’s military clothing in [59:17](https://ref.ly/Isa59:17)).
* *in generations of old*: The exodus from Egypt was an act of redemption. It showed God’s power as he made a path of escape ([Isaiah 51:10](https://ref.ly/Isa51:10)) for his people (see also [11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:9-10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Dragon (ID: `fauna:Dragon`)

## Isaiah 51:13 (id: 16486)

* *you have forgotten the LORD*: The Lord will never forget Israel (see [Isaiah 44:21](https://ref.ly/Isa44:21); [49:15](https://ref.ly/Isa49:15)), so they should remember him.
* The Creator of the world also created the nation of Israel.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 51:14 (id: 16487)

The exile in Babylon brought harsh conditions like imprisonment, starvation, and death.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Hell (ID: `place:Hell`)

## Isaiah 51:15 (id: 16488)

*I am the LORD*: The Lord uses this phrase to show he is the covenant God. It assures his people that his words are true and his actions are reliable (see [Isaiah 27:3–4](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3-Isa27:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 51:16 (id: 16489)

* *My words in your mouth*: The focus shifted back to the servant.
* *You are My people*: Israel was the covenant community.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 51:17 (id: 16491)

* *Awake, awake!*: Israel used these words in a prayer to the Lord ([Isaiah 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9)). Israel's problems were not because God was slow to act. Instead, the people were slow to believe God's promises.
* *the cup of His fury*: The Lord will give judgment in a just and measured way. He will judge in the right amount and at the right time.

    Those who come under his judgment are described as drinking from a cup of his wrath. This is a picture that shows they must fully experience the results of their sin (see also [Matthew 26:39](https://ref.ly/Matt26:39)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 51:17–23 (id: 16490)

The prophet called the people of Israel to be done with their past and to anticipate God’s future for them.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:17-23

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Oryx (ID: `fauna:Oryx`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Casting Net (ID: `realia:CastingNet`)

## Isaiah 51:20 (id: 16493)

*Your sons have fainted*: This phrase shows how terrible Jerusalem’s destruction was. Even children became weak and collapsed from hunger and suffering.

The book of Lamentations also describes this deep sorrow and pain ([Lamentations 1:13](https://ref.ly/Lam1:13), [22](https://ref.ly/Lam1:22); [2:11–12](https://ref.ly/Lam2:11-Lam2:12), [19](https://ref.ly/Lam2:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Oryx (ID: `fauna:Oryx`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`); Casting Net (ID: `realia:CastingNet`)

## Isaiah 51:22 (id: 16494)

God will defend Israel, just as he did when Assyria attacked Jerusalem ([Isaiah 38:6](https://ref.ly/Isa38:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 51:23 (id: 16495)

The Babylonians were the ones who caused destruction, war, and famine ([Isaiah 51:19](https://ref.ly/Isa51:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:23


## Isaiah 52:1 (id: 16512)

* This second call to wake up (see [Isaiah 51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17)) was in preparation for a glorious future.
* *holy city*: The new city would be holy because its citizens would be holy (see [Israel 4:3](https://ref.ly/Isa4:3)).
* *will no longer enter you*: Things that are unclean will not be permitted to enter the holy city (see [Revelation 21:27](https://ref.ly/Rev21:27)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Uncircumcised Person (ID: `keyterm:UncircumcisedPerson`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 52:3 (id: 16513)

*You were sold for nothing … you will be redeemed*: The Lord was in control when he allowed his people to be captured by their enemies. He stayed in control and could save them if he wanted to.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Isaiah 52:4 (id: 16514)

*went down to Egypt to live, then Assyria oppressed them*: The exodus from exile would resemble Israel's past exodus from Egypt.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Egypt (ID: `place:Egypt`); Assyria (ID: `place:Assyria`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Assyrians (ID: `group:Assyria`)

## Isaiah 52:5 (id: 16515)

* The oppressors would shout with joy because they thought they had defeated both Israel and its God.
* *My name is blasphemed continually all day long*: The state of God's people influences God's reputation in the eyes of the world (see [Isaiah 37:6](https://ref.ly/Isa37:6), [23](https://ref.ly/Isa37:23); [48:11](https://ref.ly/Isa48:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 52:6 (id: 16516)

Just as God revealed himself to Moses before the exodus ([Exodus 3:15](https://ref.ly/Exod3:15)), he will reveal himself in the future rescue from exile.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:6


## Isaiah 52:7 (id: 16518)

* *How beautiful on the mountains*: See [Nahum 1:15](https://ref.ly/Nah1:15); [Romans 10:15](https://ref.ly/Rom10:15).
* The good news is more than ending the exile in Babylon. It also points to the peace and salvation of the age to come ([Isaiah 54:13–14](https://ref.ly/Isa54:13-Isa54:14); see [40:9–10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:9-Isa40:10); [41:27](https://ref.ly/Isa41:27)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 52:7–12 (id: 16517)

This passage uses poetry to describe God’s promise to save his people. Their sin had separated them from God (it had broken their relationship with him). God promised to remove that separation and bring them back to himself.

The picture is of a city surrounded by enemy armies. The people inside the city are waiting for news. They hope to hear that their hero has defeated the enemy and won the battle.

Paul uses these words to speak about the good news of the Lord Jesus Christ ([Romans 10:15](https://ref.ly/Rom10:15)). He shows that this promise is fulfilled in the message about Jesus.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:7-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`)

## Isaiah 52:8 (id: 16519)

* The watchmen were those seeking news of Zion's redemption.
* *the LORD returns to Zion*: The sin of those who lived in the city had driven God away.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 52:10 (id: 16503)

*the salvation of our God*: God saving his people from shame hints at a greater victory. God will rule triumphantly everywhere (note the similarities with [Psalm 98:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps98:1-Ps98:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 52:11 (id: 16505)

Here the imagery of leaving the unclean enemy city encourages the people to purify themselves by turning away from all known sin. Those who carry home the sacred objects of the Lord need to be pure.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`)

## Isaiah 52:11–12 (id: 16504)

This is a call to escape the captivity of sin (see [Isaiah 48:20](https://ref.ly/Isa48:20) on leaving physical captivity in Babylon). See also [2 Corinthians 6:17](https://ref.ly/2Cor6:17); [2 Timothy 2:19](https://ref.ly/2Tim2:19).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:11-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`)

## Isaiah 52:12 (id: 16506)

* *you will not leave in a hurry*: At the time of the exodus, the people had to leave quickly (see [Exodus 12:11](https://ref.ly/Exod12:11), [31–36](https://ref.ly/Exod12:31-Exod12:36)). Isaiah highlighted this difference to emphasize the newness of God's plan.
* *your rear guard*: See [Exodus 13:21](https://ref.ly/Exod13:21); [14:19–20](https://ref.ly/Exod14:19-Exod14:20).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 52:13 (id: 16508)

*prosper … highly exalted*: The servant will be wise and successful, standing with the great and noble. Similar terms describe God in [Isaiah 6:1](https://ref.ly/Isa6:1) and [57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`)

## Isaiah 52:13–53.12 (id: 16507)

This is the fourth of four passages that speak about the promised servant (see study note on Isaiah 42:1–4). The Hebrew poem is carefully constructed in five three\-line stanzas.It begins with the servant’s exaltation ([52:13](https://ref.ly/Isa52:13)), proceeds to his humiliation ([52:14–53:9](https://ref.ly/Isa52:14-Isa53:9)), and ends with his exaltation ([53:10–12](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10-Isa53:12)). 

The faithful servant’s suffering would bring reconciliation between God and humans. The Lord Jesus perfectly fulfilled this prophecy (see [Isaiah 40](https://ref.ly/Isa40:1-Isa40:31); [Matthew 8:17](https://ref.ly/Matt8:17); [Acts 8:30–35](https://ref.ly/Acts8:30-Acts8:35); [Romans 10:15–17](https://ref.ly/Rom10:15-Rom10:17); [15:21](https://ref.ly/Rom15:21); [1 Peter 2:24–25](https://ref.ly/1Pet2:24-1Pet2:25)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:13-53:12
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 42:1-4 (ID: `16339@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`); Bear the Consequences Of (ID: `keyterm:BearTheConsequencesOf`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); Believe In (ID: `keyterm:BelieveIn`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Isaiah 52:14 (id: 16510)

*many were appalled*: No one expected that the rescuer would suffer and die.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:14


## Isaiah 52:14–15 (id: 16509)

Despite the servant’s greatness, the actual way he would appear on earth would surprise people.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:14-15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`)

## Isaiah 52:15 (id: 16511)

* *sprinkle many nation*: They will be amazed that a rescuer appears weak and helpless, leaving them speechless.
* What they will see and what they will understand will be in complete contrast to anything they had previously imagined.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 52:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`)

## Isaiah 53:1 (id: 16524)

* *our message*: Scholars have debated who the speaker could be. The main possibilities are:

    1. a faithful remnant of Israel, and
        2. Isaiah himself.
        Most likely, Isaiah the prophet was speaking for and with Israel.

* *the arm*: This refers to God's strength, vividly described in the previous chapters (see [Isaiah 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2); [51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5), [9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9); [52:10](https://ref.ly/Isa52:10)). It would show itself in weakness and apparent helplessness through the servant's humiliation and exaltation (see [1 Corinthians 1:27–30](https://ref.ly/1Cor1:27-1Cor1:30)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Believe In (ID: `keyterm:BelieveIn`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 53:2 (id: 16525)

* *a tender shoot … out of dry ground*: Such a plant is at risk of dying out (compare [Isaiah 37:27](https://ref.ly/Isa37:27)). It struggles to survive, much less offer anything to others.
* *no stately form or majesty*: The servant seemed to lack greatness or obvious royal splendor (see [Isaiah 52:13–15](https://ref.ly/Isa52:13-Isa52:15)).
* *to attract us*: People prefer leaders who are good\-looking and charismatic. The servant would be neither.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:2


## Isaiah 53:3 (id: 16526)

* *a man of sorrows, acquainted with grief*: The servant would suffer deeply. He would know pain and sadness from personal experience.

    He would fully experience the results of human sin and the fall (the time when the first humans disobeyed God and brought suffering into the world).

* *we esteemed Him not*: People would not see how someone weak and insignificant could help them, so they would ignore his suffering.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:3


## Isaiah 53:4 (id: 16527)

* The uncaring world might think the servant caused his own suffering, not realizing he suffered for them.
* *infirmities … sorrows*: These descriptions of the servant's humiliation contrast with those of his exaltation.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 53:5 (id: 16528)

* *He was pierced*: See also [Zechariah 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10).
* *crushed … punishment … stripes*: These were typical punishments for crimes. Sin is a crime against God.
* *brought us peace*: The Hebrew word *shalom* is usually translated as "peace." *Shalom* means experiencing wholeness in body, mind, and relationships with others. The servant would be injured so humanity can be whole and healthy in all aspects of life (see also [Isaiah 57:18](https://ref.ly/Isa57:18)). We do not need to suffer divine punishment for our sins because the servant has already done so (see [Galatians 1:4](https://ref.ly/Gal1:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`)

## Isaiah 53:6 (id: 16529)

*gone astray … turned to his own way*: These phrases are metaphors for sin (see also [Romans 3:10–18](https://ref.ly/Rom3:10-Rom3:18)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Isaiah 53:7 (id: 16531)

See [1 Peter 2:21–25](https://ref.ly/1Pet2:21-1Pet2:25) to see how Jesus Christ fulfills this prophecy.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`)

## Isaiah 53:7–8 (id: 16530)

The Ethiopian eunuch was reading this passage when Philip met him (see [Acts 8:32–33](https://ref.ly/Acts8:32-Acts8:33)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:7-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`)

## Isaiah 53:8 (id: 16532)

* *By oppression and judgment*: The servant will not receive legal protection or a proper defense.
* *cut off from the land of the living*: To die in midlife was understood to be God’s judgment.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`)

## Isaiah 53:9 (id: 16533)

* *no violence, nor was any deceit*: See [1 Peter 2:21–25](https://ref.ly/1Pet2:21-1Pet2:25).
* *with a rich man in His death* (see [Matthew 27:57–61](https://ref.ly/Matt27:57-Matt27:61)): Although the Bible often views riches as a blessing from God, it frequently condemns the rich as dishonest and oppressive. The point here might be ironic: This good man would be buried with oppressors.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`)

## Isaiah 53:10 (id: 16521)

* The servant's grief would lead to a greater good: the forgiveness and reconciliation of sinful humanity. Note also God’s good plan to prosper and exalt the servant.
* *when His soul is made a guilt offering*: The servant's suffering served as a substitute for others, similar to animal sacrifices in the Temple.
* Having many descendants and enjoying a long life are rewards for living wisely and righteously (see [Proverbs 3:2](https://ref.ly/Prov3:2); [17:6](https://ref.ly/Prov17:6); [20:7](https://ref.ly/Prov20:7)). Because the servant trusted his fate to God, he would receive eternal rewards from God, who vindicates the righteous (see [Philippians 2:9–11](https://ref.ly/Phil2:9-Phil2:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 53:10–12 (id: 16520)

The final stanza of the poem first explains why the servant suffered and was treated unjustly in the place of others; it then explains what the result of that obedience would be.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:10-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`); Bear the Consequences Of (ID: `keyterm:BearTheConsequencesOf`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`)

## Isaiah 53:11 (id: 16522)

* H*is knowledge*: This does not mean knowledge in the sense of ideas in the mind. It refers to everything the servant would live through. It includes his obedience, his suffering, and his close relationship with God.
* Because the servant obeys God fully, many people can be put right with God. This is called justification (God declaring a person to be right with him). He can do this because he will carry and bear their sins (see [Genesis 15:6](https://ref.ly/Gen15:6); [Romans 5:18–19](https://ref.ly/Rom5:18-Rom5:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`)

## Isaiah 53:12 (id: 16523)

* *a portion*: See [Philippians 2:9–11](https://ref.ly/Phil2:9-Phil2:11).
* *He was numbered with the transgressors*: *Transgressors* (Hebrew *poshe‘im*) is a stronger word than *sinners* and is important in Isaiah. It refers to those who defy lawful authority, in this case, God.
* *intercession for the transgressors*: The servant would pray for sinners while suffering (see [Exodus 32:30](https://ref.ly/Exod32:30); [Luke 23:34](https://ref.ly/Luke23:34)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 53:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Bear the Consequences Of (ID: `keyterm:BearTheConsequencesOf`)

## Isaiah 54:1 (id: 16544)

In the ancient world, a woman who had not given birth after marriage would feel ashamed. Isaiah compares Jerusalem to a woman without children who celebrates when she finally has them (see [Galatians 4:27](https://ref.ly/Gal4:27)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 54:1–3 (id: 16535)

The prophet encourages Jerusalem, likened to a barren woman, to rejoice because its fate was changing very quickly. She would have so many children that she would outgrow her home (see [Galatians 4:27](https://ref.ly/Gal4:27)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peg (ID: `realia:Peg`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Linen Strips (ID: `realia:LinenStrips`); Tent (ID: `realia:Tent`); Goat Hair Strips (ID: `realia:GoatHairStrips`); Rope (ID: `realia:Rope`); Peg (ID: `realia:Peg.2`)

## Isaiah 54:1–17 (id: 16534)

Salvation flows from the vindication of the suffering servant. The promises mentioned here go beyond the return from Babylonian exile and apply to the coming of Jesus Christ, the extension of the kingdom to the church, the benefits of the second coming of Jesus Christ as the bridegroom of the church, and the new Jerusalem.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:1-17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); Noah (ID: `person:Noah`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Peg (ID: `realia:Peg`); Linen Strips (ID: `realia:LinenStrips`); Peg (ID: `realia:Peg.2`); Tent (ID: `realia:Tent`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Craftsman (ID: `realia:Craftsman`); Rope (ID: `realia:Rope`); Goat Hair Strips (ID: `realia:GoatHairStrips`)

## Isaiah 54:1–55.13 (id: 16536)

This is an invitation to participate in the restoration to God’s favor made possible through the ministry of the promised servant.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:1-55:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); Noah (ID: `person:Noah`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Come On! (ID: `keyterm:ComeOn`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); David (ID: `person:David`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Nettle (ID: `flora:Nettle`); Peg (ID: `realia:Peg`); Fir (ID: `flora:Fir`); Linen Strips (ID: `realia:LinenStrips`); Myrtle (ID: `flora:Myrtle`); Barley (ID: `flora:Barley`); Peg (ID: `realia:Peg.2`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`); Grecian Juniper (ID: `flora:Juniper`); Tent (ID: `realia:Tent`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Craftsman (ID: `realia:Craftsman`); Rope (ID: `realia:Rope`); Coriander (ID: `flora:Coriander`); Cypress (ID: `flora:Cypress`); Goat Hair Strips (ID: `realia:GoatHairStrips`); Brier (ID: `flora:Brier`)

## Isaiah 54:3 (id: 16545)

* The descendants of Israel (compare [Isaiah 53:10](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10)) would take over other nations, fulfilling God's promise to Abraham ([Genesis 22:17](https://ref.ly/Gen22:17)) and Jacob ([Genesis 28:14](https://ref.ly/Gen28:14)).
* As desolation occurred ([Isaiah 6:11](https://ref.ly/Isa6:11); [14:21](https://ref.ly/Isa14:21)), God's people would inherit the earth and rebuild the ruined cities ([51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6); [52:10](https://ref.ly/Isa52:10); [65:17](https://ref.ly/Isa65:17); see also [Matthew 5:5](https://ref.ly/Matt5:5); [Romans 4:13](https://ref.ly/Rom4:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`)

## Isaiah 54:5 (id: 16546)

* *husband … Maker*: The Lord committed to the abandoned woman (his people in exile) as her maker ([Isaiah 44:24](https://ref.ly/Isa44:24)) and husband (see also [Isaiah 62:4–5](https://ref.ly/Isa62:4-Isa62:5); [Hosea 2:19–20](https://ref.ly/Hos2:19-Hos2:20)).
* The Redeemer transforms misery into freedom and fulfillment (see also [Isaiah 41:14](https://ref.ly/Isa41:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`)

## Isaiah 54:7–8 (id: 16547)

* The pain of separation would be brief compared to the depth of the renewal of love and compassion.
* The Lord abandoned Israel in exile for a little while because of the people’s sins.
* *everlasting kindness*: The Lord is always committed to his people, making the 70\-year exile seem brief (see [Isaiah 25:1](https://ref.ly/Isa25:1); [Hosea 2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:7-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Isaiah 54:9 (id: 16548)

The exile was similar in both drama and trauma to the flood in the time of Noah. In both instances, people sinned grievously against the Lord, but the Lord renewed his commitment to creation (after Noah’s flood) and to his people (after the exile).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Noah (ID: `person:Noah`)

## Isaiah 54:10 (id: 16537)

* God's faithful love for his people continues even when they are unfaithful.
* God's covenant of blessing assures his presence, bringing wholeness, blessing, and protection. It replaces the shame and disgrace of the exile in Babylon (see [Ezekiel 34:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:25); [37:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:26)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Isaiah 54:11 (id: 16539)

* The Lord himself would rebuild the city (see [Hebrews 11:10](https://ref.ly/Heb11:10)).
* Sapphires are a semi\-precious stone.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:11


## Isaiah 54:11–17 (id: 16538)

This describes a vision of Jerusalem renewed as a city under God's protection, a place of peace and righteousness (see [Isaiah 59:21–60:22](https://ref.ly/Isa59:21-Isa60:22)). This section provides the background for John's vision of the new Jerusalem ([Revelation 21:10–21](https://ref.ly/Rev21:10-Rev21:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:11-17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Craftsman (ID: `realia:Craftsman`)

## Isaiah 54:13 (id: 16540)

* *will be taught*: Jesus alluded to this verse in [John 6:45](https://ref.ly/John6:45).
* They will experience great peace*,* along with the benefits of God's presence and protection ([Isaiah 53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5); see [48:17–18](https://ref.ly/Isa48:17-Isa48:18); [54:10](https://ref.ly/Isa54:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 54:14 (id: 16541)

Jerusalem would again be a righteous city, secure under a government that is just and fair (see [Isaiah 1:26](https://ref.ly/Isa1:26)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Isaiah 54:15 (id: 16542)

God assures that no nation can defeat his people. The Lord promises to protect them, just as he promised Abraham ([Genesis 12:3](https://ref.ly/Gen12:3); see [Isaiah 49:25](https://ref.ly/Isa49:25)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:15


## Isaiah 54:17 (id: 16543)

* *No weapon … shall prosper … accuses … vindication*: God will protect his people in war and in the courtroom.
* *This is the heritage of the servants of the LORD*: Only the true children of God, from Israel and other nations, will enjoy the promised blessings of the future age that God establishes.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 54:17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`)

## Isaiah 55:1 (id: 16554)

* *thirsty … wine and milk*: The invitation to eat and drink is like Wisdom’s call in [Proverbs 9:5](https://ref.ly/Prov9:5). This image promises to quench thirst not only with water but also with more expensive items like wine and milk.

    John applied a similar theme to Jesus Christ ([John 4:14](https://ref.ly/John4:14); [6:27](https://ref.ly/John6:27), [35](https://ref.ly/John6:35); [7:37](https://ref.ly/John7:37); [Revelation 21:6](https://ref.ly/Rev21:6); [22:17](https://ref.ly/Rev22:17)).

* *without cost*: Reconciliation with God is for anyone and has no cost.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Come On! (ID: `keyterm:ComeOn`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Isaiah 55:1–13 (id: 16549)

This final chapter of the prophecies of comfort ([Isaiah 40–55](https://ref.ly/Isa40:1-Isa55:13)) summarizes the section’s main themes: 

* Blessing
* Covenant
* Witness
* Word
* Nations
* Glory
* Forgiveness
* Joy

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:1-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Come On! (ID: `keyterm:ComeOn`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); David (ID: `person:David`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Nettle (ID: `flora:Nettle`); Fir (ID: `flora:Fir`); Barley (ID: `flora:Barley`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`); Grecian Juniper (ID: `flora:Juniper`); Brier (ID: `flora:Brier`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Coriander (ID: `flora:Coriander`); Cypress (ID: `flora:Cypress`); Myrtle (ID: `flora:Myrtle`)

## Isaiah 55:2 (id: 16555)

*Listen carefully to Me, and eat what is good*: Responding positively to God satisfies one’s spiritual, social, and physical being (see [1:19](https://ref.ly/Isa1:19); [58:13–14](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13-Isa58:14); [Proverbs 4:10](https://ref.ly/Prov4:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Isaiah 55:3 (id: 16556)

* *come to Me; listen, so that your soul may live*: Those who obey God's word find eternal life (see also [Isaiah 55:11](https://ref.ly/Isa55:11)).
* *an everlasting covenant … promised to David*: God gave King David a special promise to preserve his royal line (see [2 Samuel 7:15–16](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:15-2Sam7:16)). God promised that David’s family line would continue forever. This promise is fulfilled in the kingship of the Messiah (God’s chosen king), Jesus Christ (see [Isaiah 9:6–7](https://ref.ly/Isa9:6-Isa9:7); [11:1–16](https://ref.ly/Isa11:1-Isa11:16); [Acts 2:22–36](https://ref.ly/Acts2:22-Acts2:36); [13:34](https://ref.ly/Acts13:34)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); David (ID: `person:David`)

## Isaiah 55:6 (id: 16558)

*while He is near*: When God kindly offers an invitation to salvation, people must respond ([Isaiah 65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1); [Jeremiah 29:13–14](https://ref.ly/Jer29:13-Jer29:14); [Hosea 5:6](https://ref.ly/Hos5:6); [10:12](https://ref.ly/Hos10:12)). Those who do not seek him during these times may never have another chance.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 55:6–9 (id: 16557)

The prophet calls for a response while the time is right.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:6-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`)

## Isaiah 55:7 (id: 16559)

* *Let the wicked man forsake his way and … return to the LORD*: True conversion means changing how we live to follow God's rules (see [Isaiah 1:16–17](https://ref.ly/Isa1:16-Isa1:17); [30:15](https://ref.ly/Isa30:15); [59:20](https://ref.ly/Isa59:20)).
* *have compassion*: God's compassion reaches those in need, wherever they are.
* *He will freely pardon*: Forgiveness of sin is key to the good news of redemption (see [Ephesians 1:7](https://ref.ly/Eph1:7); [Colossians 1:14](https://ref.ly/Col1:14); see also [Psalms 32:1](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1); [86:5](https://ref.ly/Ps86:5); [99:8](https://ref.ly/Ps99:8); [103:2–5](https://ref.ly/Ps103:2-Ps103:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 55:8–9 (id: 16560)

*My thoughts are not your thoughts*: God's plans are wonderful ([Psalm 92:5](https://ref.ly/Ps92:5); compare [Psalm 94:11](https://ref.ly/Ps94:11)). God's creations, including humans, cannot fully understand the Creator's thoughts. However, his messengers reveal insights and knowledge about some of his actions.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:8-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 55:10–11 (id: 16550)

The Creator sends rain, snow, and his word (Hebrew *dabar*). Here, *dabar* means his *will* or *plan* (see [Isaiah 14:24](https://ref.ly/Isa14:24); [40:8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8)). God's written word expresses his plan and achieves his goals. God is successful in all his actions (see [Isaiah 14:26–27](https://ref.ly/Isa14:26-Isa14:27); [46:10](https://ref.ly/Isa46:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:10-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Barley (ID: `flora:Barley`); Coriander (ID: `flora:Coriander`)

## Isaiah 55:12 (id: 16552)

* Joy is found in being redeemed from bondage.
* Godly people experience an inner peace not known to the wicked ([Isaiah 48:22](https://ref.ly/Isa48:22)).
* *mountains and hills … trees of the field*: Creation participates in the freedom of the children of God ([Isaiah 44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23); [49:11](https://ref.ly/Isa49:11), [13](https://ref.ly/Isa49:13); see [Romans 8:19–23](https://ref.ly/Rom8:19-Rom8:23)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`)

## Isaiah 55:12–13 (id: 16551)

The prophet tells the people not to focus on the past. He urges them to leave Babylon and return home.

He also calls them to see things from the viewpoint of the coming age (the new time of salvation that God is about to bring).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:12-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Nettle (ID: `flora:Nettle`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fir (ID: `flora:Fir`); Cypress (ID: `flora:Cypress`); Myrtle (ID: `flora:Myrtle`); Grecian Juniper (ID: `flora:Juniper`); Brier (ID: `flora:Brier`)

## Isaiah 55:13 (id: 16553)

* Redemption is like the transformation from desert to forest (see also [Isaiah 35:1](https://ref.ly/Isa35:1); [41:19](https://ref.ly/Isa41:19); [60:13](https://ref.ly/Isa60:13)).
* *this will make a name for the LORD*: God's glory will become increasingly visible to people as his redemption takes effect.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 55:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Nettle (ID: `flora:Nettle`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fir (ID: `flora:Fir`); Cypress (ID: `flora:Cypress`); Myrtle (ID: `flora:Myrtle`); Grecian Juniper (ID: `flora:Juniper`); Brier (ID: `flora:Brier`)

## Isaiah 56:1 (id: 16565)

* *Maintain justice and do what is right*: [Isaiah 1–39](https://ref.ly/Isa1:1-Isa39:8) emphasize the importance of justice in relationships with others and God. True godliness comes from having a character shaped by God's character, achieved by understanding and consistently applying God's word.
* *My salvation is coming soon*: This summarizes the message of [Isaiah 40–55](https://ref.ly/Isa40:1-Isa55:13), where the Lord is creating a world of harmony, peace, restoration, vindication, and the removal of enemies ([Isaiah 46:13](https://ref.ly/Isa46:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 56:1–8 (id: 16562)

This section summarizes chapters [1–55](https://ref.ly/Isa1:1-Isa55:13) with an invitation to the outcasts to participate in God’s redemption.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:1-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); To Dishonor (ID: `keyterm:Dishonor`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); A Person (ID: `person:GenericPerson`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Stone Altar (ID: `realia:StoneAltar`); Tabernacle Altar (ID: `realia:TabernacleAltar`); Temple Altar (ID: `realia:TempleAltar`); Altar (ID: `realia:Altar`)

## Isaiah 56:1–66.24 (id: 16561)

This last major division of the book of Isaiah brings together themes from chapters [1–39](https://ref.ly/Isa1:1-Isa39:8) (sin, justice, righteousness, responsibility, vengeance, and vindication) and chapters [40–55](https://ref.ly/Isa40:1-Isa55:13) (salvation and the age to come).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`)

## Isaiah 56:2 (id: 16566)

* *Blessed*: Compare [Isaiah 30:18](https://ref.ly/Isa30:18); [32:20](https://ref.ly/Isa32:20); see also [Psalms 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1); [119:1](https://ref.ly/Ps119:1); [Matthew 5:3](https://ref.ly/Matt5:3).
* The Sabbathis a sign of the covenant ([Exodus 31:13–17](https://ref.ly/Exod31:13-Exod31:17)), and is God's gift to his people. It applies to both the present age and the age to come ([Isaiah 56:4](https://ref.ly/Isa56:4), [6](https://ref.ly/Isa56:6); [58:13–14](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13-Isa58:14); see [Hebrews 4:1–13](https://ref.ly/Heb4:1-Heb4:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); To Dishonor (ID: `keyterm:Dishonor`)

## Isaiah 56:3 (id: 16568)

* Foreigners who commit themselves to the Lord would become full members of his covenant community. In earlier times, their participation had strict limits ([Deuteronomy 23:3](https://ref.ly/Deut23:3), [7–8](https://ref.ly/Deut23:7-Deut23:8)).
* *eunuch*: In the past, a man who had been emasculated was not allowed to take part fully in the community ([Deuteronomy 23:1](https://ref.ly/Deut23:1)). Both eunuchs and foreigners were seen as outside Israel’s holiness. They were considered on the edge of God’s people.

    But in the age to come, God would bring those on the margins to the center. Those who were once excluded would be welcomed and honored ([Matthew 20:16](https://ref.ly/Matt20:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** A Person (ID: `person:GenericPerson`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 56:3–7 (id: 16567)

The blessing ([56:2](https://ref.ly/Isa56:2)) would extend to eunuchs and to the Gentiles.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:3-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); To Dishonor (ID: `keyterm:Dishonor`); A Person (ID: `person:GenericPerson`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Stone Altar (ID: `realia:StoneAltar`); Tabernacle Altar (ID: `realia:TabernacleAltar`); Temple Altar (ID: `realia:TempleAltar`); Altar (ID: `realia:Altar`)

## Isaiah 56:4 (id: 16569)

The Sabbath is a sign of the covenant ([Exodus 31:13–17](https://ref.ly/Exod31:13-Exod31:17)). It shows the special relationship between the Lord and Israel.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 56:5 (id: 16570)

* *in My house and within My walls*: Those once excluded will have a place with God.
* *a memorial and a name*: Everlasting existence in God’s presence is better than the blessing of physical descendants.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** House (ID: `realia:House`)

## Isaiah 56:6 (id: 16571)

* The essential thing required for covenant fellowship is love for God ([Deuteronomy 6:5](https://ref.ly/Deut6:5); [30:20](https://ref.ly/Deut30:20); [Matthew 22:34–38](https://ref.ly/Matt22:34-Matt22:38)).
* Keeping the Sabbath is a sign of keeping the covenant itself (see [Exodus 31:13–17](https://ref.ly/Exod31:13-Exod31:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); To Dishonor (ID: `keyterm:Dishonor`)

## Isaiah 56:7 (id: 16572)

* *burnt offerings and sacrifices*: In the past, God rejected Israel’s offerings when the people were living sinful lives ([Isaiah 1:11–13](https://ref.ly/Isa1:11-Isa1:13)). Their worship did not please him because their hearts were not right.

    But God would accept offerings from righteous gentiles, that is, people from other nations who truly follow him. Being part of a certain nation does not matter if a person does not truly honor God.

* *a house of prayer for all the nations*: The Lord would open the temple to all nations (see [Isaiah 2:2–4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:2-Isa2:4)). Jesus criticized the people for disrespecting the temple and preventing it from being a house of prayer ([Matthew 21:13](https://ref.ly/Matt21:13); [Mark 11:17](https://ref.ly/Mark11:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Stone Altar (ID: `realia:StoneAltar`); Tabernacle Altar (ID: `realia:TabernacleAltar`); Temple Altar (ID: `realia:TempleAltar`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Altar (ID: `realia:Altar`)

## Isaiah 56:8 (id: 16573)

* The outcasts of Israel were people scattered among other nations due to the exile in Babylon (see also [Isaiah 11:12](https://ref.ly/Isa11:12)).
* The *others* included eunuchs and non\-Jews ([Isaiah 56:3–7](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3-Isa56:7); see also [57:19](https://ref.ly/Isa57:19); [John 10:16](https://ref.ly/John10:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 56:9 (id: 16575)

Wild animals are here a metaphor for the hostile nations surrounding Judah.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sandalwood (ID: `flora:Sandalwood`)

## Isaiah 56:9–57.13 (id: 16574)

This passage reflects on how common evil had become among God’s people. Many of them were greedy and wanted more and more for themselves ([Isaiah 56:9–12](https://ref.ly/Isa56:9-Isa56:12)). They treated righteous people with hatred and violence ([Isaiah 57:1–2](https://ref.ly/Isa57:1-Isa57:2)). They also worshiped idols instead of the true God ([Isaiah 57:3–13](https://ref.ly/Isa57:3-Isa57:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:9-57:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Play the Prostitute (ID: `keyterm:PlayTheProstitute`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`); Stone Pine (ID: `flora:Pine`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Terebinth (ID: `flora:Terebinth`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Sandalwood (ID: `flora:Sandalwood`); Doorpost (ID: `realia:Doorpost`)

## Isaiah 56:10 (id: 16563)

* The leaders of Israel failed to guide and protect God’s people from the sins that led to the exile in Babylon.
* *dreamers … loving to slumber*: See [Isaiah 29:10](https://ref.ly/Isa29:10).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`)

## Isaiah 56:11 (id: 16564)

* *shepherds with no discernment*: Israel's leaders did not know how to rule in a godly way (see also [Ezekiel 34:1–6](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:1-Ezek34:6)). In contrast, the Lord is the faithful shepherd of his people ([Isaiah 40:11](https://ref.ly/Isa40:11)).
* *they all turn to their own way*: They did not care about God or his standards for leadership (see also [Isaiah 53:6](https://ref.ly/Isa53:6)).
* *his own gain*: Compare [Isaiah 33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 56:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Isaiah 57:1 (id: 16584)

* *the presence of evil*: Judgment that would come on the wicked nations.
* *taken away*: At times, God allows the godly to die in order to protect them from harsher times to come.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Isaiah 57:1–2 (id: 16576)

This is a lament for the righteous who suffer in a wicked society.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`)

## Isaiah 57:2 (id: 16587)

The wicked would not be able to rest in peace ([57:21](https://ref.ly/Isa57:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`)

## Isaiah 57:3 (id: 16588)

*sons of a sorceress … offspring of adulterers and prostitutes*: Baal religion, which flourished in Israel before the exile (see [Isaiah 1:21–23](https://ref.ly/Isa1:21-Isa1:23)), included immoral sexual practices.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Play the Prostitute (ID: `keyterm:PlayTheProstitute`)

## Isaiah 57:4 (id: 16589)

*mocking* *… stick out your tongue*: The wicked oppressed the righteous by their insults ([Isaiah 57:1–2](https://ref.ly/Isa57:1-Isa57:2); see also [28:9](https://ref.ly/Isa28:9), [14](https://ref.ly/Isa28:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`)

## Isaiah 57:5 (id: 16590)

* *among the oaks, under every luxuriant tree*: People often used groups of trees as places to worship false gods. These tree groves were common sites for pagan worship (see [Isaiah 1:29–30](https://ref.ly/Isa1:29-Isa1:30)).
* *slaughter your children*: Some people in Israel practiced the terrible act of child sacrifice. They offered their children to false gods. This happened at certain times in Israel’s history (see [Psalm 106:37–38](https://ref.ly/Ps106:37-Ps106:38)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Terebinth (ID: `flora:Terebinth`); Stone Pine (ID: `flora:Pine`)

## Isaiah 57:6 (id: 16591)

People believed that liquid offerings and grain offerings would meet the basic needs of their gods. They thought these gifts would please the gods and cause them to show favor.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`)

## Isaiah 57:8 (id: 16592)

*uncovered your bed*: This phrase refers to sexual sin connected with idol worship. The people of Israel were deeply committed to fertility religions. These religions included sexual acts as part of their worship ceremonies.

This behavior showed how far they had turned away from the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Doorpost (ID: `realia:Doorpost`); Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`)

## Isaiah 57:11 (id: 16577)

The people were living in fear of false gods instead of fearing the Lord (see [Isaiah 8:12–13](https://ref.ly/Isa8:12-Isa8:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Isaiah 57:12 (id: 16578)

The Lord did not see the people's *righteousness* ([Isaiah 59:16](https://ref.ly/Isa59:16)) as truly righteous.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:12


## Isaiah 57:13 (id: 16579)

* *Your companies of idols … take them away*: See study note on Isaiah 40:18.
* *inherit the land*: The Hebrew word translated *land* can also refer to the whole earth (see [Psalms 25:13](https://ref.ly/Ps25:13); [37:9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:9), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps37:11), [22](https://ref.ly/Ps37:22), [29](https://ref.ly/Ps37:29), [34](https://ref.ly/Ps37:34); [Matthew 5:5](https://ref.ly/Matt5:5)).
* *My holy mountain*: God's servants will enjoy his presence in Zion (see [Isaiah 2:2](https://ref.ly/Isa2:2); [56:7](https://ref.ly/Isa56:7)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:13
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 40:18 (ID: `16300@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`)

## Isaiah 57:14–21 (id: 16580)

The Lord promises to be with the humble, heal them, and give them peace. This promise is for all citizens of Zion, both Jews and non\-Jews. The holy one offers salvation to the humble and deals fairly with the wicked.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:14-21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`)

## Isaiah 57:15 (id: 16581)

God is *high and lifted up*, yet he promises to live with the humble.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`)

## Isaiah 57:18 (id: 16582)

*but I will heal him*: Salvation is by God’s grace (see [Isaiah 19:22](https://ref.ly/Isa19:22); [30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26); [53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:18


## Isaiah 57:19 (id: 16583)

* *praise to their lips*: Those who experience God's redemption should respond with praise.
* *those far and near*: This includes both Gentiles and Jews (see [Isaiah 56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3); [John 10:16](https://ref.ly/John10:16); [Ephesians 2:17](https://ref.ly/Eph2:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 57:20 (id: 16585)

* *storm\-tossed sea … it cannot be stil**l*: People who constantly oppose God (see [Psalms 46:3](https://ref.ly/Ps46:3), [6](https://ref.ly/Ps46:6); [93:3–4](https://ref.ly/Ps93:3-Ps93:4)) do not find inner peace because they are not at peace with God ([Isaiah 30:15](https://ref.ly/Isa30:15); [32:17](https://ref.ly/Isa32:17)).
* *churn up mire and muck*: Their actions are often unproductive (see [Psalm 40:2](https://ref.ly/Ps40:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:20


## Isaiah 57:21 (id: 16586)

*no peace*: Compare [Isaiah 57:2](https://ref.ly/Isa57:2); see also [48:22](https://ref.ly/Isa48:22).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 58:1–4 (id: 16593)

This is a charge against religious Israelites who were pleased with themselves because of their religious activities.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Rams Horn (ID: `realia:RamsHorn`)

## Isaiah 58:2 (id: 16598)

*they seek me*: They may have been truly committed to a religious form, but they failed to truly meet God in their religious activities.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`)

## Isaiah 58:3 (id: 16599)

Fasting should be a time of self\-deprivation in order to focus on God and pleasing him ([Leviticus 16:29](https://ref.ly/Lev16:29); see [Zechariah 7:5](https://ref.ly/Zech7:5)). However, these people were only interested in pleasing themselves and continuing in their sins.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:3


## Isaiah 58:5 (id: 16600)

* This passage uses strong language to show the problem with the people’s fasting. Their fasting was only outward religious behavior. They did not truly repent or do what is right.

    Fasting without sincere hearts and righteous actions is empty. It is only for show.

* People wore sackcloth and put ashes on their heads to show sorrow and mourning.
* *acceptable to the LORD?*: The people wrongly believed that God would bless them if they performed the correct rituals. They thought the actions themselves would bring blessing.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rush (ID: `flora:Rush`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`)

## Isaiah 58:6–7 (id: 16601)

True fasting makes people aware of injustice and oppression. It encourages them to help those in need (see [Isaiah 42:7](https://ref.ly/Isa42:7); [Matthew 25:35–36](https://ref.ly/Matt25:35-Matt25:36); [James 1:27](https://ref.ly/Jas1:27)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:6-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Yoke (ID: `realia:Yoke`)

## Isaiah 58:8 (id: 16602)

* *your light*: The light of the age to come will shine on those who fully turn to the Lord (see [Isaiah 42:6–7](https://ref.ly/Isa42:6-Isa42:7); [60:1–3](https://ref.ly/Isa60:1-Isa60:3)). The time of divine judgment and oppression will end.
* *your healing*: The nation of Israel was wounded when they were conquered and sent into exile.
* *your rear guard*: See also [Isaiah 52:12](https://ref.ly/Isa52:12).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 58:9 (id: 16603)

* *the LORD will answer*: Compare [Isaiah 1:15](https://ref.ly/Isa1:15); see also [19:22](https://ref.ly/Isa19:22).
* *remove … the pointing of the finger and malicious talk*: God asks us to stop actions and words that harm relationships (see [Proverbs 6:12–14](https://ref.ly/Prov6:12-Prov6:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Yoke (ID: `realia:Yoke`)

## Isaiah 58:10 (id: 16594)

*your light … will be like noonday*: God would soon rescue and defend his people (see [Job 11:17](https://ref.ly/Job11:17); [Psalm 37:6](https://ref.ly/Ps37:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:10


## Isaiah 58:12 (id: 16595)

*Repairer of the Breach*: Nehemiah would later fulfill this promise (see [Nehemiah 2:17](https://ref.ly/Neh2:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Foundation (ID: `realia:Foundation`)

## Isaiah 58:13 (id: 16596)

Like the practice of fasting, the Sabbath was intended to be an expression of self\-denial and worship. It consists of delighting in the Lord, trusting him to provide for one’s needs while abstaining from work, and living in obedience to his will.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`)

## Isaiah 58:14 (id: 16597)

* As people honor God through obedience, they themselves will be honored by God
* *the heritage of your father Jacob*: This includes living with many descendants in the holy land and enjoying God's presence, blessings, and protection ([Genesis 31:3](https://ref.ly/Gen31:3); [35:9–12](https://ref.ly/Gen35:9-Gen35:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 58:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`)

## Isaiah 59:1 (id: 16611)

*not too short*: The delay in their rescue was not due to any weakness in the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 59:1–8 (id: 16605)

The people said the Lord could not rescue them, but the real issue was that they had not repented ([Isaiah 59:20](https://ref.ly/Isa59:20)) and were still sinning.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:1-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Defile (ID: `keyterm:Stain`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Exempt (ID: `keyterm:Exempt`); Spider (ID: `fauna:Spider`); Viper (ID: `fauna:Viper`); Cobra (ID: `fauna:Cobra`)

## Isaiah 59:1–20 (id: 16604)
