The Lord alone will bring salvation. Like a warrior, he will enter the world to defeat his enemies and protect his holy people.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:1-20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Defile (ID: `keyterm:Stain`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Exempt (ID: `keyterm:Exempt`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Helmet (ID: `realia:Helmet`); Breastplate (ID: `realia:Breastplate`); Spider (ID: `fauna:Spider`); Viper (ID: `fauna:Viper`); Bear (ID: `fauna:Bear`); Dove (ID: `fauna:Dove`); Robe (ID: `realia:Robe`); Cobra (ID: `fauna:Cobra`)

## Isaiah 59:2 (id: 16613)

*your sins have hidden His face from you*: The Israelites, like all people ([Romans 3:15–17](https://ref.ly/Rom3:15-Rom3:17)), had sinned. This led to separation from God, who turned away.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

## Isaiah 59:4 (id: 16614)

*calls for justice*: Israel did not practice these qualities that are part of God’s nature and that he expects from people ([Isaiah 1:21](https://ref.ly/Isa1:21); see [25:1](https://ref.ly/Isa25:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`)

## Isaiah 59:5–6 (id: 16615)

The images of deadly snakes and spiders’ webs represent plans designed to destroy others’ lives and relationships.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:5-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Spider (ID: `fauna:Spider`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Viper (ID: `fauna:Viper`); Cobra (ID: `fauna:Cobra`)

## Isaiah 59:7–8 (id: 16616)

* Paul quotes these verses when he describes the sinfulness of all people. He shows that everyone has turned away from God and does what is wrong ([Romans 3:15–17](https://ref.ly/Rom3:15-Rom3:17)).
* *ruin and destruction*: The people complained that others were treating them unfairly ([Isaiah 51:19](https://ref.ly/Isa51:19)). They said they were suffering under oppression.

    But they were also treating other people unfairly. They had become oppressors themselves.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:7-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Exempt (ID: `keyterm:Exempt`)

## Isaiah 59:9–15 (id: 16617)

Israel had a long history of turning away from God. The people often rebelled against him.

In this passage, the prophet speaks for the nation. He admits their sins and expresses deep sorrow. He says they could not grasp the light of God’s salvation (they could not understand or receive the rescue God offered).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:9-15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Dove (ID: `fauna:Dove`); Bear (ID: `fauna:Bear`)

## Isaiah 59:10 (id: 16606)

*the blind*: See [Isaiah 6:10](https://ref.ly/Isa6:10); [Deuteronomy 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:10


## Isaiah 59:11–14 (id: 16607)

The people growl and moan with longing for redemption, finally coming to terms with their sinfulness.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:11-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Dove (ID: `fauna:Dove`); Bear (ID: `fauna:Bear`)

## Isaiah 59:15–17 (id: 16608)

The Lord acts like a warrior, determined to win against evil (see [Ephesians 6:14](https://ref.ly/Eph6:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:15-17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Helmet (ID: `realia:Helmet`); Breastplate (ID: `realia:Breastplate`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Robe (ID: `realia:Robe`)

## Isaiah 59:18 (id: 16609)

*He will repay according to their deeds*: The Lord will give fair punishment to the whole earth. His justice requires judging people for the wrongs they have done (see also [Isaiah 3:9–11](https://ref.ly/Isa3:9-Isa3:11); [13:9](https://ref.ly/Isa13:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:18


## Isaiah 59:19 (id: 16610)

*where the sun sets … where it rise*: That is, in all parts of the world.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Isaiah 59:21 (id: 16612)

The new community members have the Spirit and follow God's word (see also [Ezekiel 36:24–28](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:24-Ezek36:28)). The Lord promises to renew the children through his Spirit. He will bring them into a covenant of obedience and love for the Lord ([Joel 2:28](https://ref.ly/Joel2:28); [Acts 2:39](https://ref.ly/Acts2:39)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 60:1–3 (id: 16629)

God will come to be with his people and will transform Zion’s destiny from the darkness as black as night caused by sin and judgment.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`)

## Isaiah 60:3 (id: 16642)

The transformation of all nations and mighty kings will be so radical that all kingdoms will take notice and be drawn to God through his people (see also [Revelation 21:24](https://ref.ly/Rev21:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`)

## Isaiah 60:5–7 (id: 16643)

Zion will recover its losses from war through the wealth of many lands. Foreign nations will eagerly contribute ([Isaiah 60:11](https://ref.ly/Isa60:11); [61:6](https://ref.ly/Isa61:6); [66:12](https://ref.ly/Isa66:12); see [Revelation 21:26](https://ref.ly/Rev21:26)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:5-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Nebaioth (ID: `person:Nebaioth`); Ephah (ID: `place:Ephah`); Sheba (ID: `place:Sheba.2`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Kedar (ID: `person:Kedar`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Midian (ID: `place:Midian`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Stone Altar (ID: `realia:StoneAltar`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Tabernacle Altar (ID: `realia:TabernacleAltar`); Frankincense (ID: `flora:Frankincense`); Camel (ID: `fauna:Camel`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Temple Altar (ID: `realia:TempleAltar`); Altar (ID: `realia:Altar`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Isaiah 60:6 (id: 16644)

* Midian and Ephah were Midianite tribes located southeast of Israel.
* Sheba was a region in southern Arabia (see [1 Kings 10:1–2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:1-1Kgs10:2)). It was known for its frankincense ([Jeremiah 6:20](https://ref.ly/Jer6:20)).
* These were real nations, and their restoration would be a historical reality, not just an idealistic vision. Many nations would submit to and worship the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Ephah (ID: `place:Ephah`); Sheba (ID: `place:Sheba.2`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Midian (ID: `place:Midian`); Frankincense (ID: `flora:Frankincense`); Camel (ID: `fauna:Camel`)

## Isaiah 60:7 (id: 16645)

* Kedar was a tribe in Arabia ([Isaiah 21:16–17](https://ref.ly/Isa21:16-Isa21:17)).
* Nebaioth was a tribe of Ishmael's descendants.
* *I will adorn My glorious house*: God's glory will be more evident than in Solomon’s Temple (see [Isaiah 6:1–5](https://ref.ly/Isa6:1-Isa6:5); [Haggai 2:9](https://ref.ly/Hag2:9); [Revelation 21:11](https://ref.ly/Rev21:11), [22](https://ref.ly/Rev21:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Nebaioth (ID: `person:Nebaioth`); Kedar (ID: `person:Kedar`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Stone Altar (ID: `realia:StoneAltar`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Tabernacle Altar (ID: `realia:TabernacleAltar`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Temple Altar (ID: `realia:TempleAltar`); Altar (ID: `realia:Altar`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Isaiah 60:9 (id: 16646)

*He has glorified you*: See [Revelation 21:26](https://ref.ly/Rev21:26).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Ship (ID: `realia:Ship`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Tarshish (ID: `place:Tarshish`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Isaiah 60:10 (id: 16631)

*their kings will serve you*: God's people, once humiliated ([Isaiah 2:11](https://ref.ly/Isa2:11)), will be honored.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`)

## Isaiah 60:10–16 (id: 16630)

Those who previously oppressed the people of God will one day participate in restoring the holy city (see [Isaiah 2:2–4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:2-Isa2:4); [19:18–25](https://ref.ly/Isa19:18-Isa19:25); [Nehemiah 2:8](https://ref.ly/Neh2:8); [Amos 9:12](https://ref.ly/Amos9:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:10-16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Lebanon (ID: `place:Lebanon`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Force to Serve (ID: `keyterm:EnticedToServe`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Laurustinus (ID: `flora:Laurustinus`); Fir (ID: `flora:Fir`); Grecian Juniper (ID: `flora:Juniper`); Cypress (ID: `flora:Cypress`); Phoenician Juniper (ID: `flora:JuniperTree`)

## Isaiah 60:11 (id: 16632)

There are two likely reasons why the gates remain open at night (see also [Revelation 21:25](https://ref.ly/Rev21:25)): 

1. A lot of people will enter the city ([Isaiah 60:8–10](https://ref.ly/Isa60:8-Isa60:10)), so the gates must stay open.
2. The citizens of Zion will not fear enemies because the Lord protects them ([Isaiah 60:18](https://ref.ly/Isa60:18)); the remaining nations will serve the Lord and his people ([60:9](https://ref.ly/Isa60:9), [12](https://ref.ly/Isa60:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`)

## Isaiah 60:12 (id: 16633)

In the coming age, nations that refuse to obey God will be destroyed.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Force to Serve (ID: `keyterm:EnticedToServe`)

## Isaiah 60:13 (id: 16634)

The temple was God's sanctuary, the symbol of his presence on earth. Believers now are the temple of God by the indwelling Spirit ([1 Corinthians 3:16](https://ref.ly/1Cor3:16)). We still await the fullness of God’s presence ([Revelation 21:22](https://ref.ly/Rev21:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Laurustinus (ID: `flora:Laurustinus`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Lebanon (ID: `place:Lebanon`); Fir (ID: `flora:Fir`); Grecian Juniper (ID: `flora:Juniper`); Cypress (ID: `flora:Cypress`); Phoenician Juniper (ID: `flora:JuniperTree`)

## Isaiah 60:15 (id: 16635)

*an everlasting pride, a joy from age to age*: This would be a major change from the exile in Babylon. Fulfillments of this prophecy include: 

* the return of the jews from exile,
* the arrival of Jesus Christ, and
* the inclusion of non\-Jewish people in the church.

The final fulfillment awaits the glorious second coming of Jesus Christ, which will bring in the new Jerusalem.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:15


## Isaiah 60:17 (id: 16637)

* *gold … silver … bronze … iron*: People use these metals for decoration and strength (compare [Isaiah 54:11](https://ref.ly/Isa54:11); [Revelation 21:15–21](https://ref.ly/Rev21:15-Rev21:21)).
* Peace and righteousness will create harmony and blessings among people and with God.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Isaiah 60:17–20 (id: 16636)

These verses describe the future greatness of Zion (compare [Revelation 21:10–22:4](https://ref.ly/Rev21:10-Rev22:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:17-20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Isaiah 60:18 (id: 16638)

*you will name your walls Salvation and your gates Praise*: People will enter the city of salvation through gates of praise to the Lord for his great salvation.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 60:19–20 (id: 16639)

The sun and moon will seem dull compared to the brightness of God's presence (see also [Isaiah 9:2](https://ref.ly/Isa9:2); [30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26); [Zechariah 2:5](https://ref.ly/Zech2:5); [Revelation 21:23–24](https://ref.ly/Rev21:23-Rev21:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:19-20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 60:21 (id: 16640)

* The citizens of Zion will be righteous*,* enjoying a right relationship with God and with others ([Revelation 21:27](https://ref.ly/Rev21:27)).
* *the land:* Or *all the earth* ([Isaiah 54:5](https://ref.ly/Isa54:5); [62:7](https://ref.ly/Isa62:7); [65:17](https://ref.ly/Isa65:17); [66:22](https://ref.ly/Isa66:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`)

## Isaiah 60:22 (id: 16641)

*I am the LORD*: The Lord reveals himself as the covenant God of Israel. His identity guarantees he will fulfill his promises (see [Isaiah 27:3–4](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3-Isa27:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 60:22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 61:1 (id: 16649)

* The presence of God's Spirit guarantees the servant's mission will succeed (see [Isaiah 11:1–5](https://ref.ly/Isa11:1-Isa11:5); [42:1](https://ref.ly/Isa42:1)).
* The servant will help and heal the marginalized, the poor, and those with broken hearts (see [Isaiah 25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Smear (ID: `keyterm:Smear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 61:1–3 (id: 16647)

The servant announces the good news that his work will restore Zion. The Lord Jesus used these words to describe his own mission ([Luke 4:18–19](https://ref.ly/Luke4:18-Luke4:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Smear (ID: `keyterm:Smear`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Terebinth (ID: `flora:Terebinth`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`)

## Isaiah 61:2 (id: 16650)

* The time of the Lord’s favor is the day of God’s salvation (see [Isaiah 49:8](https://ref.ly/Isa49:8)).
* *the day of our God’s vengeance*: God turns his wrath against the enemies of his people. It will be a day of righteous vengeance (see [Isaiah 34:8](https://ref.ly/Isa34:8); [63:3–4](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3-Isa63:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`)

## Isaiah 61:3 (id: 16651)

* Putting ashes on one's head showed mourning (note the change from the judgment in [Isaiah 3:16–25](https://ref.ly/Isa3:16-Isa3:25)). Mourning would turn into joy. In Hebrew, the words for *crown of beauty* and *ashes* form a wordplay.
* *oil of joy*: A face covered with oil showed blessing and well\-being (see [Psalms 45:8](https://ref.ly/Ps45:8); [133:2](https://ref.ly/Ps133:2)).
* The remnant would begin as small shoots. They would seem weak and unimportant at first.

    But over time, they would grow strong and large, like great oak trees (see [Isaiah 60:21](https://ref.ly/Isa60:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Terebinth (ID: `flora:Terebinth`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`)

## Isaiah 61:6 (id: 16652)

*priests … ministers*: The nation would fulfill its original calling ([Exodus 19:6](https://ref.ly/Exod19:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 61:7 (id: 16653)

The people had faced God's wrath for their sins ([Isaiah 40:2](https://ref.ly/Isa40:2)). Now, they would receive a double share of God’s blessing.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`)

## Isaiah 61:8 (id: 16654)

Those who belong to the community of God’s people are to imitate the character of God. God loves justice (see [Micah 6:8](https://ref.ly/Mic6:8)) and hates wrongdoing ([Isaiah 33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`)

## Isaiah 61:9 (id: 16655)

* *Their descendants*: The agreement will be effective for all future generations.
* The Lord has blessed them to keep his promise to Abraham ([Genesis 12:1–2](https://ref.ly/Gen12:1-Gen12:2); see [Isaiah 65:23](https://ref.ly/Isa65:23)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`)

## Isaiah 61:10 (id: 16648)

*garments of salvation … robe of righteousness*: God will joyfully clothe his people with the gifts of salvation and righteousness (see [Isaiah 51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5); [56:1](https://ref.ly/Isa56:1)), like a bridegroom or a bride (see [49:18](https://ref.ly/Isa49:18); [62:5](https://ref.ly/Isa62:5); [Jeremiah 2:32](https://ref.ly/Jer2:32); [7:34](https://ref.ly/Jer7:34); [16:9](https://ref.ly/Jer16:9); [25:10](https://ref.ly/Jer25:10); [33:11](https://ref.ly/Jer33:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 61:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Robe (ID: `realia:Robe`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Priesthood (ID: `keyterm:Priesthood`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Isaiah 62:1–12 (id: 16656)

The servant here commits himself to proclaiming the good news continuously until the glory of Zion is realized.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:1-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Appoint (ID: `keyterm:Appoint`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Turban (ID: `realia:Turban.2`); Must (ID: `realia:Must`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Grain (ID: `flora:Grain`); Crown (ID: `realia:Crown`); Standard (ID: `realia:Standard`)

## Isaiah 62:2 (id: 16659)

* The true beauty and glory of God's people would not be in earthly treasures or a rebuilt city. Instead, it would shine from their righteousness. Others would be attracted by this divine gift.
* A new name shows the change in Zion's status (see [Isaiah 62:4](https://ref.ly/Isa62:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 62:4 (id: 16660)

* *Forsaken*: During the exile in Babylon, the Lord left his people to face the judgment they deserved ([Isaiah 54:7](https://ref.ly/Isa54:7)).
* *His bride*: The people will be "married" to the Lord, symbolizing a close covenant relationship ([Isaiah 54:5](https://ref.ly/Isa54:5); [Hosea 1–3](https://ref.ly/Hos1:1-Hos3:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 62:6–7 (id: 16661)

* The *watchmen* probably represent the prophets.
* *they will never be silent day or night*: They will continually seek the full restoration and blessing of Zion, unlike the destructive watchmen of [Isaiah 56:10](https://ref.ly/Isa56:10).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:6-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Appoint (ID: `keyterm:Appoint`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 62:8 (id: 16662)

* The Lord swore an oath just as he did when he had sworn an oath:

    + to Noah ([Genesis 8:20–9:17](https://ref.ly/Gen8:20-Gen9:17)),
        + to Abraham ([Genesis 22:16](https://ref.ly/Gen22:16); see also [Luke 1:73](https://ref.ly/Luke1:73); [Hebrews 6:13](https://ref.ly/Heb6:13)), and
        + to David ([Psalm 89:3](https://ref.ly/Ps89:3)).
* *Never again will I give*: To punish and cleanse a small group of his people, God allowed the Assyrians and Babylonians to destroy them (see [Isaiah 6:12–13](https://ref.ly/Isa6:12-Isa6:13)). He promised not to do this again.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Must (ID: `realia:Must`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`); Grain (ID: `flora:Grain`)

## Isaiah 62:9 (id: 16663)

The people would enjoy the results of their work ([Isaiah 65:13](https://ref.ly/Isa65:13), [22](https://ref.ly/Isa65:22); see [Leviticus 23:39–40](https://ref.ly/Lev23:39-Lev23:40); [Deuteronomy 14:22–26](https://ref.ly/Deut14:22-Deut14:26)). This is the opposite of what happened when God judged them (see [Deuteronomy 28:38–40](https://ref.ly/Deut28:38-Deut28:40); [Amos 5:11](https://ref.ly/Amos5:11); [Micah 6:14–15](https://ref.ly/Mic6:14-Mic6:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 62:10 (id: 16658)

* *highway*: See also [Isaiah 40:3](https://ref.ly/Isa40:3); [57:14](https://ref.ly/Isa57:14).
* A banner for all nations to see serves as a signal. It directs foreigners to come to Zion for God's salvation. Earlier, a similar signal called for the nations to attack Judah ([Isaiah 5:26](https://ref.ly/Isa5:26)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Standard (ID: `realia:Standard`)

## Isaiah 62:10–12 (id: 16657)

This call to prepare for God’s coming salvation combines elements from all parts of Isaiah: 

* the proclamation (the banner, see [Isaiah 5:26](https://ref.ly/Isa5:26)),
* the preparation of the road ([Isaiah 40:3](https://ref.ly/Isa40:3); [57:14](https://ref.ly/Isa57:14)),
* God's arrival with his reward ([Isaiah 40:5](https://ref.ly/Isa40:5), [9–10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:9-Isa40:10)), and
* the holy and redeemed people ([Isaiah 35:8–10](https://ref.ly/Isa35:8-Isa35:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 62:10-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Standard (ID: `realia:Standard`)

## Isaiah 63:1 (id: 16672)

* The Lord’s robes are red with blood ([Isaiah 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3); compare [Revelation 19:13](https://ref.ly/Rev19:13)).
* The name *Bozrah* (Hebrew *botsrah*) is a play on the verb *batsar*, which means “gather grapes.”

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Edom (ID: `place:Edom`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Bozrah (ID: `place:Bozrah.2`)

## Isaiah 63:1–6 (id: 16664)

The judgment of Edom serves as an example of how God judges all nations (see also [Isaiah 34:5–15](https://ref.ly/Isa34:5-Isa34:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:1-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Winepress (ID: `realia:Winepress`); Defile (ID: `keyterm:Stain`); Bozrah (ID: `place:Bozrah.2`); Edom (ID: `place:Edom`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`)

## Isaiah 63:2 (id: 16673)

The term *red* (Hebrew ’adom) is a wordplay on the name *Edom.*

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Winepress (ID: `realia:Winepress`)

## Isaiah 63:4 (id: 16674)

* *the day of vengeance*: See study note on Isaiah 34:8.
* *redemption*: See study note on Isaiah 43:1.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:4
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 34:8 (ID: `16214@TyndaleStudyNotes`); Isaiah 43:1 (ID: `16358@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`)

## Isaiah 63:6 (id: 16675)

*made them drunk*: This phrase describes God’s judgment. The wicked nations are pictured as people who drink strong wine and cannot stand straight.

They are forced to drink from the cup of God’s wrath (his anger against sin). This image shows that they cannot escape his punishment ([Isaiah 49:26](https://ref.ly/Isa49:26); [51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17), [21](https://ref.ly/Isa51:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:6


## Isaiah 63:7 (id: 16677)

*loving devotion … His praiseworthy acts … the many good things*: The whole history of God’s care for his people demonstrates his compassion, provision, and protection.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Isaiah 63:7–14 (id: 16676)

The prophet reflects on Israel’s past, how God showed his unfailing love and how his people alternatively rebelled against him, suffered hardship, returned to the Lord, and experienced his blessing.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:7-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Moses (ID: `person:Moses`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); An Angel (ID: `deity:Angel`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`)

## Isaiah 63:8 (id: 16678)

* *They are surely My people*: God's ownership and commitment to his people were central to the agreement (see [Isaiah 43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1)).
* *who will not be disloyal*: The Lord expected his people to honor their relationship with him, unlike other nations, whose history is full of betrayal ([Genesis 3–11](https://ref.ly/Gen3:1-Gen11:32)). But Israel also betrayed their God ([Isaiah 63:10](https://ref.ly/Isa63:10)).
* *their Savior*: God saved and protected his people (see [Isaiah 43:3](https://ref.ly/Isa43:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:8


## Isaiah 63:9 (id: 16679)

* *He too was afflicted*: God felt the pain of his suffering people, and their suffering caused him grief. Later, Jesus suffered for all humanity.
* *He redeemed them*: For example, see [Exodus 12:25–33](https://ref.ly/Exod12:25-Exod12:33); [14:10–31](https://ref.ly/Exod14:10-Exod14:31); [23:20–23](https://ref.ly/Exod23:20-Exod23:23).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); An Angel (ID: `deity:Angel`)

## Isaiah 63:10 (id: 16665)

* *But they rebelled*: See also [Isaiah 1:2](https://ref.ly/Isa1:2), [4](https://ref.ly/Isa1:4); [59:1–15](https://ref.ly/Isa59:1-Isa59:15); [Psalms 78](https://ref.ly/Ps78:1-Ps78:72), [106](https://ref.ly/Ps106:1-Ps106:48).
* Persistent disobedience and unbelief grieved God's Holy Spirit ([Psalm 106:33](https://ref.ly/Ps106:33); [Matthew 12:32](https://ref.ly/Matt12:32); [Mark 3:29](https://ref.ly/Mark3:29)).
* *became their enemy*: Examples include Israel’s period of wandering in the wilderness (see the book of Numbers), the period of the judges (see the book of Judges), and the period leading up to the exile (see the book of Jeremiah).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`)

## Isaiah 63:14 (id: 16666)

God provided his people with rest after leading them into the promised land (see [Isaiah 63:9](https://ref.ly/Isa63:9); [Joshua 1:13](https://ref.ly/Josh1:13); [22:4](https://ref.ly/Josh22:4); [23:1](https://ref.ly/Josh23:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`)

## Isaiah 63:15–64.12 (id: 16667)

The prophet’s reflections on the past ([Isaiah 63:7–14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7-Isa63:14)) inspire him to pray that God would now rescue his people as he has done before.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:15-64:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Abraham (ID: `person:Abraham`); House (ID: `realia:House`)

## Isaiah 63:16 (id: 16668)

*our Father*: God is described as a Father to his people. His care and faithfulness to his children are stronger and more secure than their family or national ancestry.

Their true identity does not depend only on being descendants of Abraham. It depends on belonging to God as his children (see [Isaiah 9:6](https://ref.ly/Isa9:6); [64:8](https://ref.ly/Isa64:8); [Deuteronomy 32:6](https://ref.ly/Deut32:6); [Psalm 68:5](https://ref.ly/Ps68:5); [Malachi 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mal2:10); [Matthew 6:9–15](https://ref.ly/Matt6:9-Matt6:15); [Romans 8:15–17](https://ref.ly/Rom8:15-Rom8:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Abraham (ID: `person:Abraham`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 63:17 (id: 16669)

* The prophet understands that God has the power to keep his people faithful and asks why God has allowed them to stray from His path.
* *Return, for the sake of Your servants*: God also has the power to bring his people back to him ([Ezekiel 36:25–27](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:25-Ezek36:27)) and to defeat all their enemies.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`)

## Isaiah 63:18 (id: 16670)

* The people were supposed to be holy*,* but their lack of holiness had led to destruction.
* The holy place was the temple, which the Babylonians destroyed in 586 BC ([Isaiah 64:11](https://ref.ly/Isa64:11); [2 Kings 25:1–21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:1-2Kgs25:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`)

## Isaiah 63:19 (id: 16671)

Israel was constantly turning from God and then being punished by him.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:19


## Isaiah 64:5–6 (id: 16681)

*we remain in our sins … like something unclean*: See also [Isaiah 59:1–15](https://ref.ly/Isa59:1-Isa59:15). The only hope for salvation and transformation is through Jesus Christ (see [Romans 7:25–8:11](https://ref.ly/Rom7:25-Rom8:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 64:5-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Defilement (ID: `keyterm:Defilement`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`)

## Isaiah 64:7 (id: 16682)

* *You have hidden Your face from us*: See [Isaiah 1:15](https://ref.ly/Isa1:15); [30:20](https://ref.ly/Isa30:20); [59:2](https://ref.ly/Isa59:2).
* *delivered us into the hand of our iniquity*: Because the people insisted on sinning, God left them to perish in their sinful ways (see [Romans 1:24](https://ref.ly/Rom1:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 64:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

## Isaiah 64:8 (id: 16683)

clay … potter: See also [Isaiah 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16); [45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9); [Romans 9:20–21](https://ref.ly/Rom9:20-Rom9:21).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 64:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 64:10 (id: 16680)

*holy cities*: All the cities of Judah were considered holy to God. However, the temple in the host city of Jerusalem was the most holy place.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 64:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 65:1–2 (id: 16684)

The Lord gave the people of Israel many chances to seek him ([Isaiah 55:6](https://ref.ly/Isa55:6); [58:2](https://ref.ly/Isa58:2)), but they did not do so.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:1-2


## Isaiah 65:1–66.24 (id: 16685)

Here, the Lord responds to the prayer in [Isaiah 63:7–64:12](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7-Isa64:12).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:1-66:24

## Isaiah 65:3 (id: 16696)

Sacred gardens were a part of the fertility religion of Baal.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Brick (ID: `realia:Brick`)

## Isaiah 65:4 (id: 16697)

* *spending nights in secret places*: God had banned the practice of necromancy (consulting dead spirits; see [Isaiah 8:19–22](https://ref.ly/Isa8:19-Isa8:22); [47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9); [Deuteronomy 18:9–11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:9-Deut18:11)).
* *the meat of pigs and polluted broth from their bowls*: See [Isaiah 66:3–4](https://ref.ly/Isa66:3-Isa66:4); [Leviticus 11:7](https://ref.ly/Lev11:7); [Deuteronomy 14:3](https://ref.ly/Deut14:3), [8](https://ref.ly/Deut14:8).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Pig (ID: `fauna:Pig`); Jackal (ID: `fauna:Jackal`)

## Isaiah 65:5 (id: 16698)

*do not come near me, for I am holier than you!*: The people of Israel used the words of ritual purification found in the law of Moses. They spoke as if they were clean and obedient to God.

But they also practiced worship influenced by pagan religions. Their actions did not match their words.

Because of this hypocrisy, God was deeply displeased. The text says they were like a bad smell in his nostrils. This image shows how offensive their behavior was to him (see [Isaiah 1:10–15](https://ref.ly/Isa1:10-Isa1:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`)

## Isaiah 65:6 (id: 16699)

* *Behold, it is written before Me*: God is fully committed to carrying out his plan.
* The godly asked if the Lord would remain silent forever ([Isaiah 64:12](https://ref.ly/Isa64:12)). God answered that he would not stay silent. He would address the wicked before restoring the righteous remnant.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:6


## Isaiah 65:7 (id: 16700)

The people of Israel practiced their pagan religion at sacred shrines on mountains and hills (see [1 Kings 14:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:23); [Hosea 4:13](https://ref.ly/Hos4:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

## Isaiah 65:8–16 (id: 16701)

The Lord planned to spare and reward a remnant of righteous people.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:8-16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Fortune (ID: `deity:Fortune`); Destiny (ID: `deity:Destiny`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Sharon Plain (ID: `place:Sharon.2`); Tribe of Judah (ID: `group:Judah`); Surely! (ID: `keyterm:Surely`); Valley of Achor (ID: `place:AchorValley`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Judah (ID: `person:Judah.4`); Oath (ID: `keyterm:Oath`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Must (ID: `realia:Must`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Vine (ID: `flora:Vine`)

## Isaiah 65:10 (id: 16686)

Sharon was a fertile and beautiful marshy region southwest of Mount Carmel (see [Isaiah 33:9](https://ref.ly/Isa33:9)). The valley of Achor in the vicinity of Jericho was associated with the curse on Achan ([Joshua 7:24–26](https://ref.ly/Josh7:24-Josh7:26)). It would become a place of blessing ([Hosea 2:15](https://ref.ly/Hos2:15)). These western and eastern regions might represent the renewal of the whole land.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sharon Plain (ID: `place:Sharon.2`); Valley of Achor (ID: `place:AchorValley`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Isaiah 65:11 (id: 16687)

*Fortune* and *Destiny* are names of pagan gods (*Gad, Meni*) believed to bring good luck and decide a person's future.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fortune (ID: `deity:Fortune`); Destiny (ID: `deity:Destiny`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`)

## Isaiah 65:12 (id: 16688)

*destine*: This verb (Hebrew *manithi*) is a wordplay on *Meni* (see study note on Isaiah 65:11). Only God controls human and national destinies.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:12
* **Associated Articles:** Isaiah 65:11 (ID: `16687@TyndaleStudyNotes`)


## Isaiah 65:13–15 (id: 16689)

The difference between the wicked and God's servants affects all parts of life. God punishes the wicked but blesses those who truly serve him (see [Isaiah 65:17–25](https://ref.ly/Isa65:17-Isa65:25)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:13-15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Oath (ID: `keyterm:Oath`)

## Isaiah 65:15 (id: 16690)

The new name given to the faithful symbolizes a new identity and a closer relationship with the Lord (see also [Isaiah 62:2](https://ref.ly/Isa62:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Oath (ID: `keyterm:Oath`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 65:16 (id: 16691)

*the former troubles*: Compare [Revelation 21:4](https://ref.ly/Rev21:4).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Surely! (ID: `keyterm:Surely`)

## Isaiah 65:17 (id: 16692)

The new heavens and new earth will be a place of righteousness (see also [2 Peter 3:13](https://ref.ly/2Pet3:13); [Revelation 21](https://ref.ly/Rev21:1-Rev21:27)). Paul described believers in Christ as already being part of the new creation (see [2 Corinthians 5:17](https://ref.ly/2Cor5:17); [Galatians 6:15](https://ref.ly/Gal6:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:17


## Isaiah 65:18–19 (id: 16693)

Jerusalem here symbolizes God’s eternal kingdom ([Revelation 21:1–4](https://ref.ly/Rev21:1-Rev21:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:18-19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 65:24 (id: 16694)

God will be present with his people (see also [Isaiah 19:22](https://ref.ly/Isa19:22); [30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19); compare [1:15](https://ref.ly/Isa1:15); [59:2](https://ref.ly/Isa59:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:24


## Isaiah 65:25 (id: 16695)

The snakes represent evil ([Isaiah 27:1](https://ref.ly/Isa27:1); [Genesis 3](https://ref.ly/Gen3:1-Gen3:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:25

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Snake (ID: `fauna:Snake`); Wolf (ID: `fauna:Wolf`)

## Isaiah 66:1 (id: 16709)

* *My throne … My footstool*: God's kingdom extends over all creation (see [Isaiah 40:22](https://ref.ly/Isa40:22); [Matthew 5:34–035](https://ref.ly/Matt5:34-Matt5:35)).
* *house … place of repose*: The entire universe is God's home, so humans cannot confine him to a building (see [1 Kings 8:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:27)).
* Stephen quoted [Isaiah 66:1–2a](https://ref.ly/Isa66:1-Isa66:2) in his final sermon ([Acts 7:49–50](https://ref.ly/Acts7:49-Acts7:50)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Throne (ID: `realia:Throne`); House (ID: `realia:House`)

## Isaiah 66:2 (id: 16713)

* God is pleased to live with people who are humble and have contrite hearts. A humble person does not think too highly of themselves. A contrite heart is a heart that is truly sorry for sin ([Isaiah 57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15); [Psalm 51:17](https://ref.ly/Ps51:17)).
* *who trembles at My word*: The humble and contrite submit themselves to God’s will, but the proud resist it.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 66:3 (id: 16714)

* *delighted in their abominations*: This refers to pagan worship practices.
* *slaughters an ox … blesses an idol*: Some wicked people offered sacrifices as the law of Moses required. But they continued to sin and worship idols. Because of this, their sacrifices were no better than pagan offerings in God’s sight (see [Isaiah 1:11–17](https://ref.ly/Isa1:11-Isa1:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Detested Thing (ID: `keyterm:DetestedThing`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Frankincense (ID: `flora:Frankincense`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`); Pig (ID: `fauna:Pig`); Jackal (ID: `fauna:Jackal`)

## Isaiah 66:6 (id: 16715)

* *listen to the voice from the temple*: This is similar to the situation in [Ezekiel 9](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:1-Ezek9:11) when God's executioner began to destroy the wicked people of Jerusalem.
* *repaying His enemies what they deserve!*: God would punish the members of his own people who refused to obey him. He would give them the judgment they deserved.

    Because they continued in sin and rejected God, they had become his enemies.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 66:7 (id: 16716)

*in labor … delivered a boy*: See [Isaiah 9:6–7](https://ref.ly/Isa9:6-Isa9:7); [54:1–3](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1-Isa54:3).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:7


## Isaiah 66:8 (id: 16717)

*Can a country be born in a day or a nation be delivered in an instant?*:Zion is pictured as a woman giving birth to many children. This image shows that the city will quickly grow in population (see [Isaiah 49:19–20](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19-Isa49:20); [54:1–3](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1-Isa54:3); compare [1:8–9](https://ref.ly/Isa1:8-Isa1:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 66:9 (id: 16718)

The Lord promised that he would surely recreate his holy nation.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 66:12 (id: 16702)

*a river*: The people who lost peace and prosperity during the exile in Babylon will receive it back in abundance (see [Isaiah 48:18](https://ref.ly/Isa48:18)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 66:13 (id: 16703)

*will I comfort you*: Compare [Isaiah 40:1](https://ref.ly/Isa40:1).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`)

## Isaiah 66:14 (id: 16704)

* *flourish like grass*: Contrast [Isaiah 40:7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7); [64:6](https://ref.ly/Isa64:6).
* God's enemies included disobedient people from foreign nations and from within Israel (see [Isaiah 61:2](https://ref.ly/Isa61:2) and [66:6](https://ref.ly/Isa66:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Grass (ID: `flora:Grass`); Green Vegetation (ID: `flora:GreenVegetation`)

## Isaiah 66:15–16 (id: 16705)

* *the Lord will come with fire*: God will appear with furious judgment against his enemies (see [Psalm 7:13](https://ref.ly/Ps7:13)). By contrast, he will reveal his glory to his people (see [Isaiah 40:5](https://ref.ly/Isa40:5), [10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:10)).
* God will judge all creation as a part of the process of renewing the earth (see [Isaiah 24:1–4](https://ref.ly/Isa24:1-Isa24:4); [65:17](https://ref.ly/Isa65:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:15-16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Chariot (ID: `realia:Chariot`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`)

## Isaiah 66:17 (id: 16706)

People who dedicate (*consecrate*) and cleanse (*purify*) themselves through idol worship are offensive to God (see [Isaiah 1:28–31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:28-Isa1:31); [65:4](https://ref.ly/Isa65:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mouse (ID: `fauna:Mouse`); Pig (ID: `fauna:Pig`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Jackal (ID: `fauna:Jackal`)

## Isaiah 66:18–23 (id: 16707)

When God reveals his glory to all people, the nations join in God’s plan of redemption. They can even serve as priests and Levites before him. The godly from all nations last from generation to generation as they serve the living God.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:18-23

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Tubal (ID: `place:Tubal`); Sabbath (ID: `keyterm:Sabbath`); Israelites (ID: `group:Israel`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Levi (ID: `person:Levi.3`); New Moon Festival (ID: `keyterm:NewMoonFestival`); Greece (ID: `place:Greece`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Tarshish (ID: `place:Tarshish`); Lud (ID: `person:Lud`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Tribe of Levi (ID: `group:Levi`); Put (ID: `place:Put`); Chariot (ID: `realia:Chariot`); Mule (ID: `fauna:Mule`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Camel (ID: `fauna:Camel`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`); Wagon (ID: `realia:Wagon`)

## Isaiah 66:19 (id: 16708)

The sign announces God's glory to all nations ([Isaiah 66:20](https://ref.ly/Isa66:20)). God will establish a righteous and faithful new people made up of godly Gentiles together with faithful Israelites ([Revelation 15:3–4](https://ref.ly/Rev15:3-Rev15:4); see also [Acts 2](https://ref.ly/Acts2:1-Acts2:47)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Tubal (ID: `place:Tubal`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Lud (ID: `person:Lud`); Greece (ID: `place:Greece`); Gentiles (ID: `group:Gentile`); Tarshish (ID: `place:Tarshish`); Put (ID: `place:Put`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`)

## Isaiah 66:21 (id: 16710)

That some of the Gentiles would serve as priests and Levites shows the removal of God’s distinction between Hebrews and Gentiles (see [Isaiah 56:7](https://ref.ly/Isa56:7); see also [Galatians 6:15](https://ref.ly/Gal6:15); [Ephesians 4:11–13](https://ref.ly/Eph4:11-Eph4:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Levi (ID: `person:Levi.3`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Tribe of Levi (ID: `group:Levi`)

## Isaiah 66:22 (id: 16711)

* *will endure before Me*: God's promise to Abraham was certain ([Genesis 17:7](https://ref.ly/Gen17:7); see also [Galatians 3:8](https://ref.ly/Gal3:8), [14](https://ref.ly/Gal3:14)).
* *your name will endure*: The identity of this new people will last forever (see [Isaiah 59:21](https://ref.ly/Isa59:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Isaiah 66:24 (id: 16712)

* Isaiah gives a final warning of the severity of God’s judgment. The book begins and ends with the condemnation of those who have rebelled (see [Isaiah 1:2–4](https://ref.ly/Isa1:2-Isa1:4)).
* God's judgment on wicked people will cause extreme fear because they will have no hope or relief from suffering (see also [Matthew 5:22](https://ref.ly/Matt5:22); [25:41](https://ref.ly/Matt25:41); [Mark 9:47–48](https://ref.ly/Mark9:47-Mark9:48); [Revelation 20:11–15](https://ref.ly/Rev20:11-Rev20:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 66:24

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Worm (ID: `fauna:Maggot`)
